TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二元线性规划的班级排课数学模型，旨在自动化排课任务并最小化所有教室的剩余座位数。以泰国KMUTT数学系为案例，结果表明该模型显著优于当前的人工排课方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排课是一项繁琐且耗时的任务，人工排课效率低下且难以优化教室资源利用，存在大量座位浪费的问题。

Method: 采用二元线性规划（Binary Linear Programming）构建排课数学模型，以最小化所有教室的剩余座位数为优化目标。

Result: 以泰国KMUTT数学系为案例进行实证研究，模型结果较当前人工排课方法有显著改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的二元线性规划模型能够有效自动化排课过程，并大幅减少教室座位浪费，提升资源利用效率。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a mathematical model for class scheduling using binary linear programming with the aims to automate the tedious task and to minimize the leftover seats distributed over all the classrooms. A case study at the Department of Mathematics, King Mongkut's University of Technology Thonburi (KMUTT), Thailand, is also presented. The result from our model is shown to have improved significantly over the current manual scheduling method.

</details>


### [220] [Separation of Regular Co-Radiant Sets via Inner Approximating Sequences, Radial Profile Functions and Applications to Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10727)
*Fernando García-Castaño,Miguel Angel Melguizo-Padial*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分析共辐射集的新框架，基于新的正则性概念，引入内逼近序列和径向轮廓函数两个工具，扩展了经典理论，实现了更广泛的正则共辐射集分离定理、近似效率标量化、以及变分不等式中的误差界与精确罚原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典共辐射集分析依赖于范数基的存在，限制了其适用范围。本文旨在建立不依赖范数基的统一框架，涵盖分离与优化等核心问题。

Method: 1. 提出新的正则性定义；2. 引入内逼近序列，用于正则性的序贯刻画并证明一般分离定理；3. 引入径向轮廓函数，刻画范数基存在性、正则性（结合锥的固体性假设），并用于近似效率的标量化；4. 建立分离残差的变分解释，得到径向深度误差界和度量误差界。

Result: 1. 得到正则共辐射集的一般分离定理；2. 给出范数基存在性和正则性的新刻画；3. 无需额外结构假设即可实现近似效率的标量化；4. 建立了分离残差的径向深度误差界和度量误差界，得到平衡问题和变分不等式中近似径向可行性的充分条件，以及在残差零水平集上的精确罚原理。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架统一并推广了共辐射集理论，为分离定理、优化标量化及变分分析提供了更通用的工具，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We develop a new framework for the analysis of co-radiant sets based on a new notion of regularity. This framework extends the classical theory beyond co-radiant sets admitting a norm-base and provides a unified setting for separation and optimization. We introduce two new tools: inner approximating sequences and the radial profile function. The former provides a sequential approach to regularity and is the key ingredient in the proof of a general separation theorem for regular co-radiant sets, extending the existing separation theory. The latter is defined for arbitrary co-radiant sets and yields new characterizations of the existence of norm-bases, together with sequence-based characterizations of regularity under a solidness assumption on the associated cone. It also gives rise to a scalar function leading to scalarization results for approximate efficiency without additional structural assumptions on the underlying co-radiant set. Finally, we provide a variational interpretation of the separation residual by establishing radial-depth and metric error bounds. These yield sufficient conditions for approximate radial feasibility in equilibrium problems and variational inequalities, and an exact penalization principle on the zero level set of the residual.

</details>


### [221] [Parameter-Free Cubic-Regularized Newton Method: Sharp Complexity and Generalized Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10741)
*Shaoying Fang,Naoki Marumo,Akiko Takeda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需先验问题参数的自适应三次正则化牛顿法，在比现有二阶方法更弱的广义光滑性条件下，推导出了寻找(ε, δ)-二阶稳定点的最优复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二阶优化方法通常需要预先知道问题依赖的参数（如Lipschitz常数），这在实际中难以获得。此外，已有参数无关的二阶方法复杂度保证不够理想。本文旨在设计一种完全参数无关且具有更优复杂度保证的三次正则化牛顿法变体。

Method: 在广义光滑性条件‖∇³f(x)‖ ≤ L₀ + L₁‖∇f(x)‖下，推导了Taylor型不等式并证明了其与广义光滑性条件的等价性，基于此设计了参数无关的自适应三次正则化牛顿法，并分析了其寻找(ε, δ)-二阶稳定点的oracle复杂度。

Result: 获得了比现有参数无关二阶方法更优的复杂度界；当L₁=0时，该界在对L₀、ε、δ及初始函数值间隙的依赖上达到最优（仅差对数项）。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在更弱的广义光滑性假设下，参数无关的三次正则化牛顿法仍能达到最优复杂度，拓展了该类方法的适用场景和理论保证。

Abstract: We analyze a variant of the cubic-regularized Newton method for nonconvex optimization. This variant is parameter-free in that it requires no prior knowledge of problem-dependent parameters. Under the generalized smoothness condition $\|\nabla^3 f(x)\| \leq L_0 + L_1 \|\nabla f(x)\|$, we derive an oracle complexity bound for finding an $(\varepsilon, δ)$-second-order stationary point. This assumption is weaker than the generalized smoothness conditions used in existing analyses of second-order methods, while the complexity bound improves upon existing guarantees for parameter-free second-order methods. In particular, when $L_1 = 0$, the bound matches the optimal dependence on $L_0$ as well as on $\varepsilon$, $δ$, and the initial function value gap, up to additive logarithmic terms. To establish this bound, we derive Taylor-type inequalities and prove their equivalence to the generalized smoothness condition.

</details>


### [222] [Enhancing Presolve in Mixed Integer Programming by Combining Probing and Dual Fixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10767)
*Zhao-Wei Wang,Wei-Kun Chen,Yu-Hong Dai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在MIP求解器中将探测（probing）与对偶固定（dual fixing）两种预求解技术相结合，通过在对探测框架中嵌入对偶固定以推导更强变量蕴含关系，并开发改进的对偶固定方法以检测更多变量缩减，在HiGHS求解器上的实验证明了所提技术的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIP求解器中探测和对偶固定是两种强大的预求解技术，但两者通常是独立应用的，如何有效结合二者以进一步提升求解器性能是一个有价值的研究问题。

Method: 首先将对偶固定嵌入到探测框架中，利用对偶固定推导出更多有用的变量蕴含关系，增强探测能力；然后开发一种改进的对偶固定技术，使更多变量能够被固定，并利用探测框架来检测这些变量约简。

Result: 在MIPLIB 2017基准实例上使用开源MIP求解器HiGHS进行测试，实验结果表明所提出的两种技术具有潜力，能够有效结合探测和对偶固定以提升求解性能。

Conclusion: 将探测与对偶固定相结合的技术能够有效增强预求解效果，推导出更强的变量蕴含关系并实现更多变量固定，从而提升MIP求解器的整体性能。

Abstract: Probing and dual fixing are two powerful presolve techniques in mixed integer programming (MIP) solvers. Probing tentatively sets some binary variables to 0 or 1, applies linear constraint based domain propagation techniques to derive better variable bounds, and extracts useful information such as stronger variable implications and better global variable bounds. Dual fixing attempts to fix variables to lower or upper bounds while ensuring that at least one optimal solution is retained, as long as the problem was feasible. In this paper, we investigate how to combine the two approaches in MIP solvers to achieve a better performance. In particular, we first embed dual fixing into the probing framework, deriving more useful variables' implications for enhancing the capability of probing. Then, we develop an improved dual fixing technique where more variable fixings can be applied, and use the probing framework to detect the reductions. Computational results on the MIPLIB 2017 benchmark instances demonstrate the potential of the two proposed techniques in combining probing and dual fixing on the open-source MIP solver HiGHS.

</details>


### [223] [RED-SEGA:Resilient Decentralized Stochastic Proximal Optimization with Gradient Sketching over Time-Varying Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10791)
*Jinhui Hu,Guo Chen,Huaqing Li,Liang Ran,Hexi Liang,Tao Han,Tingwen Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对拜占庭容错的去中心化随机优化问题，提出了一种基于梯度 sketching 的方差缩减方法 Gossip-SEGA 及其鲁棒扩展 RED-SEGA，在数据稀疏高维任务（如图像去模糊）中无需随机数据采样即可实现方差缩减，并在时变网络上证明了线性收敛速率和拜占庭鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒去中心化方差缩减随机梯度算法依赖随机数据采样，在数据稀缺但高维的任务（如图像去模糊）中效率低下，亟需一种不依赖随机采样的替代方差缩减方案。

Method: 将梯度 sketching 技术整合到去中心化随机近端梯度下降与 gossip 通信中，提出 Gossip-SEGA；为应对拜占庭攻击，将其中的加权平均替换为范数惩罚近似，得到 RED-SEGA。

Result: 推导了 RED-SEGA 在时变网络上达成共识及线性收敛速率的充分条件，并通过数值实验验证了算法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于梯度 sketching 的方差缩减方法能有效替代随机数据采样方式，实现拜占庭鲁棒的去中心化优化，适用于数据稀疏高维任务。

Abstract: Variance reduction is indispensable in Byzantine-resilient decentralized stochastic optimization over multi-agent systems (MASs) for its ability to mitigate gradient noise and thereby enhance the resilient aggregation process. However, most existing Byzantine-resilient decentralized variance-reduced (VR) stochastic gradient algorithms rely on random data sampling, which proves inefficient in data-scarce yet high-dimensional tasks, for instance, image deblurring. This paper pursues an alternative technical line that achieves variance reduction via gradient sketching. To this end, we first formulate a class of structural risk minimization (SRM) problems, where the local objectives are not necessarily decomposable and their gradients may be unavailable. To solve the SRM problems in a decentralized manner, we integrate a gradient-sketching technique into decentralized stochastic proximal gradient descent with gossip communication to propose a decentralized VR stochastic gradient algorithm, dubbed Gossip-SEGA.Since Gossip-SEGA does not provide any resilience against Byzantine attacks, a resilient extension of Gossip-SEGA,namely RED-SEGA,is developed via replacing the weighted average in Gossip-SEGA by a norm-penalized approximation. Theoretically, we derive sufficient conditions for both consensus (among reliable agents) and linear convergence rate of RED-SEGA over time-varying networks. The effectiveness and resilience of the proposed algorithms are validated through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [224] [On the Existence of Almost Periodic Solutions with Applications to Global Entrainment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10838)
*Iasson Karafyllis,Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了动力系统中几乎周期解的存在性与稳定性，将周期系统的相关结果推广到几乎周期情形，并应用于非线性时不变控制系统的全局诱引问题。证明了无外力系统的局部指数稳定性与对小输入输入-状态稳定性可保证对小幅几乎周期输入的全局诱引，并在Lotka-Volterra系统中得到了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的周期系统理论成果无法直接适用于更一般的几乎周期系统，本研究旨在推广这些结果，以解决非线性控制系统中几乎周期输入的全局诱引问题。

Method: 利用局部指数稳定性与输入-状态稳定性（ISS）两种性质，证明它们联合足以保证系统对几乎周期输入具有全局诱引特性；并将结果应用于具有Volterra-Lyapunov稳定交互矩阵的Lotka-Volterra系统。

Result: 证明了局部指数稳定性与对小输入的ISS可确保全局诱引到小幅几乎周期输入；Lotka-Volterra系统在给定条件下满足该性质；所有结论可进一步推广到一致回归情形。

Conclusion: 本文为几乎周期系统的全局诱引问题提供了统一的理论框架，拓展了已有周期系统成果，并在生态模型中得到验证。

Abstract: This paper provides two results that are useful in the study of the existence and the stability properties of almost periodic solutions for a given dynamical system. The obtained results are generalizations of recent results for periodic systems and are applied to the global entrainment problem in nonlinear time-invariant control systems. It is shown that local exponential stability for the unforced system and input-to-state stability with respect to small inputs can guarantee global entrainment to small almost periodic inputs. In this way, global entrainment is shown in Lotka-Volterra systems with a Volterra-Lyapunov stable interaction matrix. All results can be extended to the uniformly recurrent case.

</details>


### [225] [Optimization on Affine-Transversal Hilbert Submanifolds: Part I -- Theoretical Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10861)
*Yongcun Song,Luhao Xue,Xiaoming Yuan,Hangrui Yue*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为Hilbert空间中仿射-横截Hilbert子流形上的优化问题建立了理论基础，提出了投影诱导黎曼度量及显式梯度公式，并设计了具有严格可行迭代和收敛保证的黎曼线搜索与信赖域算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典黎曼优化依赖于隐式切空间Riesz表示，计算复杂。本文旨在建立一种可直接计算算子评估的变量度量框架，使可行集为非线性流形与仿射子空间交集的优化问题能够应用欧氏空间标准技术，同时保持几何结构以支持收敛分析。

Method: 1) 发展了几何与分析工具集：切空间刻画、投影算子、隐式回缩算子；2) 推导了提升目标泛函导数的弱形式表达式；3) 引入投影诱导黎曼度量，其诱导范数与范数一致等价；4) 构造投影诱导梯度作为显式公式的精确黎曼梯度；5) 在此基础上设计黎曼线搜索与信赖域算法并给出收敛性分析。

Result: 建立了仿射-横截Hilbert子流形上优化问题的完整理论基础，实现了用算子评估替代隐式Riesz表示，提出了具有严格可行迭代和收敛保证的黎曼线搜索与信赖域算法框架。

Conclusion: 该理论框架使得欧氏空间的标准优化技术可直接应用于仿射-横截Hilbert子流形上的约束优化问题，为实际算法设计提供了兼具可计算性与几何保真度的新途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the theoretical foundations for the generic optimization problem in a Hilbert space whose feasible set is an affine-transversal Hilbert submanifold given by the intersection of a nonlinear manifold and an affine subspace. We develop the geometric and analytical toolkit, such as the tangent-space characterization, projection operators, and implicit retraction operators, to essentially ensure the feasibility of iterates for algorithmic design. We also derive weak-form expressions for the derivatives of lifted objective functionals. In particular, we introduce a projection-induced Riemannian metric whose induced norm is uniformly equivalent to the ambient norm, under which the projection-induced gradient becomes an exact Riemannian gradient with an explicit formula. This construction replaces the implicit tangent-space Riesz representation underlying classical Riemannian optimization with directly computable operator evaluations, yielding a practical variable-metric framework for algorithmic design while preserving the geometric structure required for convergence analysis. With these theoretical foundations, it becomes possible to apply standard techniques in Euclidean spaces to design algorithms with strictly feasible iterates for the optimization problem on an affine-transversal Hilbert submanifold. We also propose the theoretical frameworks for algorithmic design on affine-transversal Hilbert submanifolds by showcasing the Riemannian line-search and trust-region algorithms with rigorous convergence analysis.

</details>


### [226] [Stochastic Dynamic Barrier Perturbed Gradient Methods for Nonconvex Simple Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10957)
*Mohammad Mahdi Ahmadi,Jincheng Cao,Aryan Mokhtari,Erfan Yazdandoost Hamedani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对随机简单双层优化中下层约束导致的对偶乘子无界问题，提出SDBPG算法及其变体，首次在随机非凸-非凸简单双层优化中给出了显式的(ε_f,ε_g)-驻点保证，样本复杂度达到O(ε^{-4})和O(ε^{-6})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机简单双层优化中，下层约束引发的对偶乘子在下层驻点附近可能无界，导致有界对偶分析失效并破坏随机梯度估计的稳定性，现有方法缺乏对此问题的有效处理。

Method: 提出SDBPG（随机动态障碍扰动梯度法），一种单循环方法，通过对偶公式的自适应扰动来正则化退化问题；进一步提出PR-SDBPG（惩罚正则化变体）消除稀有访问假设，以及VR-PR-SDBPG（方差缩减变体）提升样本复杂度。

Result: 在稀有访问假设下，SDBPG在O(max{ε_f^{-2},ε_g^{-2}})次迭代内找到(ε_f,ε_g)-驻点，上、下层目标样本梯度复杂度分别为O(ε^{-4})和O(ε^{-6})，其中ε=max{ε_f,ε_g}。

Conclusion: 这是首次在随机非凸-非凸简单双层优化中给出显式的(ε_f,ε_g)-驻点保证，所提方法有效解决了对偶乘子无界的关键挑战。

Abstract: We study stochastic simple bilevel optimization with smooth, possibly nonconvex upper- and lower-level objectives accessed only through stochastic gradient oracles. A key challenge is that the dual multiplier induced by the lower-level constraint may become unbounded near lower-level stationary points, invalidating bounded-dual analyses and destabilizing stochastic gradient estimates. To address this, we propose \emph{Stochastic Dynamic Barrier Perturbed Gradient} (SDBPG), a single-loop method that adaptively perturbs the dual formulation to regularize this degeneracy. The perturbation stabilizes the multiplier and yields controlled bias and variance even near the lower-level stationarity region. Under a mild rare-visit assumption, SDBPG finds an $(ε_f,ε_g)$-stationary point in $\mathcal{O}(\max\{ε_f^{-2},ε_g^{-2}\})$ iterations, with sample gradient complexities $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$ and $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-6})$ for the upper- and lower-level objectives where $ε=\max\{ε_f,ε_g\}$. We further develop PR-SDBPG, a penalty-regularized variant that eliminates the rare-visit assumption, and VR-PR-SDBPG, which improves the resulting sample complexities entirely through variance reduction. To our knowledge, these are the first explicit $(ε_f,ε_g)$-stationarity guarantees for stochastic nonconvex-nonconvex simple bilevel optimization.

</details>


### [227] [Actor-Critic Learning for Extended Mean Field Control with Deterministic Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11005)
*Ziheng Cheng,Xin Guo,Huyên Pham,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于连续时间扩展平均场控制问题的无模型强化学习框架，通过确定性反馈策略和策略梯度公式，避免了随机核优化，并结合粒子近似、测度相关神经网络和时间差分学习，在随机Cucker-Smale一致控制等实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在扩展平均场设置中存在关键局限性，需要优化随机核，而本文旨在建立无模型的确定性策略梯度框架来克服这些限制。

Method: 首先建立参数化McKean-Vlasov动力学的无模型灵敏度公式，推导出Wasserstein空间上的优势率函数策略梯度；然后引入依赖于状态、动作和联合状态-动作分布的局部值和优势率表示；最后提出基于鞅的学习原理和连续时间深度确定性策略梯度算法，结合粒子近似、测度相关神经网络、TD学习和探索策略。

Result: 在随机Cucker-Smale一致控制和最优清算等数值实验中，该方法表现出高效性、稳定性和鲁棒性，特别是在控制分布显式依赖的问题上。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了连续时间扩展平均场控制问题的无模型强化学习框架，为处理控制分布依赖问题提供了可行的算法路径。

Abstract: This paper develops a model-free reinforcement learning framework for continuous--time extended mean field control problems, where both the dynamics and reward may depend on the joint distribution of states and controls. We adopt deterministic feedback policies, under which the state--action distribution is induced directly as a push--forward of the state law. This avoids optimization over stochastic kernels and bypasses key limitations of existing approaches in extended mean field settings.
  We first establish a model--free sensitivity formula for parameterized McKean--Vlasov dynamics and use it to derive a deterministic policy gradient formula expressed through an advantage--rate function on the Wasserstein space. We then refine this formula by introducing local value and advantage--rate representations that depend on the state, action, and joint state--action distribution, yielding a policy gradient that includes both action derivatives and measure--derivative terms with respect to the control distribution. These characterizations lead to a martingale--based learning principle and motivate a continuous--time deep deterministic policy gradient algorithm combining particle approximations, measure--dependent neural networks, temporal--difference learning, and exploration in either action or parameter space.
  Numerical experiments on stochastic Cucker--Smale consensus control and optimal liquidation with trade crowding demonstrate the efficiency, stability, and robustness of the proposed method, including problems with explicit dependence on the control distribution.

</details>


### [228] [Last-Iterate Convergence of Single-Loop Stochastic Methods for Constrained Convex-Concave Minimax Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11056)
*Taoli Zheng,Jiajin Li,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带方差随机噪声的约束光滑凸-凹极小极大优化问题，提出基于扰动框架的PS-EG和PS-OGDA两种单循环方法，分别实现已知优化时长下的O(T^{-1/4})和未知时长下的O(T^{-1/5})最后迭代收敛率，无约束场景下PS-EG达到O(T^{-1/4})的随时收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准随机外梯度法(S-EG)和随机乐观梯度下降上升法(S-OGDA)在随机梯度噪声存在时，即使在简单双线性问题上也无法保证最后迭代收敛，这严重限制了这类方法在实际应用中的可靠性。

Method: 提出一种扰动框架，将原始凸-凹问题正则化为强凸-强凹问题；对扰动后问题应用S-EG和S-OGDA得到PS-EG和PS-OGDA；通过先推导扰动问题鞍点平方距离的收敛性，再转化为受限原始-对偶间隙的保证；针对已知/未知优化时长分别设计固定扰动和衰减扰动的变体。

Result: 已知优化时长：PS-EG和PS-OGDA均达到O(T^{-1/4})的最后迭代收敛率；未知优化时长：在闭凸可行集上达到O(T^{-1/5})；无约束场景下PS-EG达到O(T^{-1/4})的梯度范数收敛率。

Conclusion: 所提出的扰动框架有效克服了随机梯度噪声导致的最后迭代不收敛问题，为带约束凸-凹极小极大优化提供了理论保证更优的实用算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we study last-iterate convergence of stochastic first-order methods for constrained smooth convex--concave minimax optimization under the standard bounded-variance stochastic oracle. A fundamental challenge is that the last iterates of vanilla stochastic extragradient (S-EG) and stochastic optimistic gradient descent--ascent (S-OGDA) may fail to converge in the presence of stochastic gradient noise, even for simple bilinear problems. To overcome this difficulty, we introduce a simple perturbation framework that regularizes the original convex--concave problem into a strongly convex--strongly concave one. Applying S-EG and S-OGDA to the perturbed problem yields two simple single-loop methods, referred to as perturbed S-EG (PS-EG) and perturbed S-OGDA (PS-OGDA).
  We establish last-iterate convergence by first deriving convergence in terms of the squared distance to the saddle point of the perturbed problem and then translating this estimate into guarantees for the restricted primal--dual gap. Based on this framework, we establish two types of convergence guarantees. When the optimization horizon is known \emph{a priori}, both PS-EG and PS-OGDA achieve an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ last-iterate convergence rate for the restricted primal--dual gap, which coincides with the standard primal--dual gap on compact feasible domains. When the optimization horizon is unknown, we develop an anytime variant based on diminishing perturbations and diminishing stepsizes. For general closed convex feasible sets, both PS-EG and PS-OGDA achieve an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/5})$ last-iterate convergence rate for the restricted primal--dual gap. Furthermore, in the unconstrained setting, PS-EG admits a sharper $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ anytime convergence rate in terms of the gradient norm.

</details>


### [229] [The optimal rate of convergence in mean field control via recoupled shadow flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11062)
*Sebastian Munoz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明N粒子随机最优控制问题的值函数以经验测度速率一致收敛到平均场控制问题的值函数，对于d≥3为N^{-1/d}，d=2为N^{-1/2}√(log N)，在1-Wasserstein距离下仅需Lipschitz连续性。证明采用控制论方法构建路径Fokker-Planck流（影子流），通过最优传输反复与粒子重新耦合。一维情况下发现经验测度基准并非最优，合作粒子可达到更优的4/7多项式指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Daudin、Delarue和Jackson猜想的最优收敛速率，移除其所需的半凹性假设，并将结果推广至更一般的Lipschitz连续平均场成本函数。

Method: 从每个N粒子控制实现出发构建路径Fokker-Planck流（影子流），通过最优传输反复与粒子重新耦合；一维情形结合影子流的预期校正、实现合作的Gibbs定律以及最优性的Schrödinger基态估计。

Result: 对d≥2证明最优收敛速率N^{-1/d}（d≥3）和N^{-1/2}√(log N)（d=2），且速率在该类中达到最优；一维情形发现最优多项式指数为4/7，优于独立样本和自由放置点的量化精度。

Conclusion: 平均场控制问题的经验测度收敛速率在d≥2时已达最优，但一维情况下粒子合作可超越该基准，揭示降维后粒子间协同的新优势。

Abstract: We prove that the value functions of the $N$-particle stochastic optimal control problem converge uniformly to the value function of the corresponding mean field control problem at the empirical-measure rate ($N^{-1/d}$ for $d\geq3$ and $N^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log N}$ for $d=2$), for mean field costs that are merely Lipschitz continuous in the $1$-Wasserstein distance. For $d\geq2$ the rate is optimal in this class, which covers problems whose mean field optimizers are neither unique nor stable. This proves the rate conjectured by Daudin, Delarue and Jackson, and removes the semiconcavity hypothesis made there. The proof is control-theoretic: from each realization of an $N$-particle control we build a pathwise Fokker--Planck flow -- a shadow flow -- which, repeatedly recoupled to the particles by optimal transport, shadows the empirical measure at the empirical-measure rate. The same rate holds with additive common noise, uniformly in its intensity. Finally, in dimension one we discover that the empirical-measure benchmark is not optimal: cooperating particles beat it, and the optimal polynomial exponent is $4/7$, strictly between the accuracy of independent samples and that of quantization by freely placed points. The proofs combine an anticipating correction of the shadow flow, a Gibbs law implementing the cooperation, and a Schrödinger ground-state estimate for the optimality.

</details>


### [230] [Weak Observability Characterization for Abstract Wave Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11202)
*Nisrine Charaf,Faouzi Triki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了由斜自伴算子 iA₀（A₀ 为自伴椭圆算子）生成的二阶无穷维演化系统的弱可观测性，建立了基于谱强制性的谱刻画，证明了A₀的预解估计与系统弱可观测性之间的直接联系，并将结果应用于矩形域上波动方程在不同观测区域几何构型下的弱可观测性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为二阶无穷维演化系统（由斜自伴算子生成）的弱可观测性建立统一的谱框架，揭示椭圆算子A₀的预解估计与系统弱可观测性之间的内在联系，并填补该领域缺乏系统谱刻画方法的空白。

Method: 引入观测算子的谱强制性概念，建立其与合适预解估计的等价性；通过频域方法、预解估计与傅里叶分析，证明Hautus型谱可观测性估计；在矩形域上结合具体几何构型进行傅里叶分析验证。

Result: 证明了A₀的预解不等式蕴含系统A的Hautus型谱可观测性估计，从而保证弱可观测性；在矩形域波动方程上给出了不同观测区域构型下的显式弱可观测性估计。

Conclusion: 建立了预解不等式、谱强制性与弱可观测性之间的统一谱框架，为更广泛的无穷维系统可观测性分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the weak observability of second-order infinite-dimensional evolution systems generated by skew-adjoint operators of the form $iA_0$, where $A_0$ is a self-adjoint elliptic operator. We first establish a spectral characterization of weak observability by introducing the notion of spectral coercivity for the observation operator and proving its equivalence to a suitable resolvent estimate. Our main result reveals a direct link between resolvent estimates for the elliptic operator $A_0$ and the weak observability of the associated evolution generator $A$. More precisely, we prove that a resolvent inequality for $A_0$ implies a Hautus-type spectral observability estimate for $A$, which guarantees the weak observability of the system. This provides a unified spectral framework for weak observability based on the coercivity properties of the observation operator. As an application, we establish explicit weak observability estimates for the wave equation on a rectangular domain under several geometric configurations of the observation region. The analysis combines frequency-domain methods, resolvent estimates, and Fourier analysis, yielding new insights into the interplay between resolvent inequalities, spectral coercivity, and weak observability in infinite-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [231] [Modular Autonomous Transit: From Vehicle Modularity to Deployment-Ready Transit Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11232)
*Dongyang Xia,Shadi Sharif Azadeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文总结了模块化自动驾驶车辆（MAV）在运营层面（线路、多式联运、网络、随机实时控制及充电基础设施）的优势，基于四项科学研究，面向科技公司、公交运营商、自动驾驶开发者等受众。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明MAV的核心优势不仅在于物理耦合，更在于其模块化单元（MAU）带来的多维度运营效率提升，并为行业决策者提供科学依据。

Method: 基于Dr. Xia和Dr. Sharif Azadeh的四项关于模块化公交运营的科学研究，其中包含一项基于NExT MAU技术数据的充电基础设施研究。

Result: 模块化自动驾驶车辆（MAV）由模块化自动驾驶单元（MAU）组成，在线路运营、多式联运服务、网络运营、随机实时控制以及电动化运营与充电基础设施方面均具有优势。

Conclusion: MAV的模块化特性创造了超越物理耦合的运营优势，适用于多种交通运营场景。

Abstract: Audience: Tech companies such as NExT leadership; public transport operators; autonomous transit developers; charging partners; public authorities, and industrial partners considering pilots. Content: The strongest aspect of Modular Autonomous Vehicles (MAVs) is not only that vehicles can physically couple. A MAV is composed of Modular Autonomous Units (MAUs), and modularity creates operating advantages across line-level operations, intermodal services, network-level operations, stochastic real-time control, and electrified vehicle operations and charging infrastructure. Research basis and disclaimer: The brief summarizes scientific findings of four studies conducted by Dr. Xia and Dr. Sharif Azadeh on modular transit operations, including a charging infrastructure study using technical data associated with NExT MAUs.

</details>


### [232] [An augmented Lagrangian algorithm for constrained nonlinear least-squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11239)
*Pierre Borie,Fabian Bastin,Stéphane Dellacherie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合增广拉格朗日函数与梯度投影法，处理混合非线性与线性约束的非线性最小二乘问题的算法，并证明了全局收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性最小二乘问题的求解方法在处理同时包含非线性和线性约束的混合约束时效果有限，需要一种既能有效处理非线性约束又能直接处理线性约束的算法。

Method: 将非线性约束通过增广拉格朗日函数引入目标函数，线性约束保持显式处理；每次迭代利用梯度投影技术近似求解线性约束子问题；并采用增广拉格朗日Hessian的结构化近似。

Result: 证明了算法的全局收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了性能。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效求解混合非线性与线性约束的非线性最小二乘问题，具有良好的全局收敛性和实际表现。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for solving nonlinear least-squares problems subject to a mix of nonlinear and linear constraints. The nonlinear constraints are handled by reformulating the objective as the augmented Lagrangian function while linear constraints are handled directly. Each iteration consists of approximately solving a linearly constrained problem by means of a gradient projection technique. Our approach also involves a structured approximation of the augmented Lagrangian Hessian. We show global convergence of the method and assess the performance through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [233] [Optimal Control of Pandemic Dynamics via Model Predictive Control: A Health-Economic Trade-off Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11306)
*Lokman Rachid Melhani,Lars Grune,Antonino Sferlazza,Dominique Persano Adorno,Filippo D'Ippolito,Alberto Firenze*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出经济模型预测控制（MPC）框架应用于扩展SEIR-V模型，通过动态调整社交接触水平（传播率β）和疫苗接种力度，在最小化死亡人数、医疗容量违规和经济损失的多目标成本函数下优化流行病控制。研究发现预测时域N=35天最优，闭环系统对该参数稳健；并证明"锤子与舞蹈"策略是数学最优解，通过转向点（turnpike）分析建立了最优工作点的实际渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在流行病控制中，严格的封控措施虽能抑制传播但造成巨大经济破坏，而完全放开则导致医疗系统崩溃和高死亡率。现有研究缺乏在冲突的社会经济目标下提供严格数学最优解的决策支持工具，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用经济模型预测控制（MPC）框架，基于扩展SEIR-V（易感-暴露-感染-康复-疫苗接种）仓室模型，将控制问题构建为约束非线性优化问题。控制器动态调整两类操作变量：社交互动水平（传播率β）和疫苗接种努力，以最小化包含死亡惩罚、医疗容量违规和经济损失的复合成本函数。进行预测时域N的敏感性分析，以及开环和闭环转向点（turnpike）分析。

Result: 预测时域N=35天使实现成本最小化；闭环系统对时域选择具有鲁棒性。"锤子与舞蹈"策略被证明是数学最优解：最优轨迹锚定到唯一的抑制转向点（最大封锁），将住院人数驱动到无病平衡点，然后逐步重新开放经济。通过转向点论证建立了最优工作点的实际渐近稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个具有严格数学基础的流行病政策决策支持工具，理论上证明了"锤子与舞蹈"策略的最优性，并为MPC在流行病控制中的应用提供了全面的性能分析与稳定性保证。

Abstract: This paper addresses the optimal control of epidemic dynamics under conflicting socio-economic objectives. We propose an economic Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework, applied to an extended SEIR-V (Susceptible-Exposed-Infected-Recovered-Vaccinated) compartmental model to govern the spread of an infectious disease while minimizing economic disruption. The control problem is formulated as a constrained nonlinear optimization problem, in which the controller dynamically adjusts social interaction levels (transmission rate beta) and vaccination efforts to minimize a composite cost function that penalizes fatalities, healthcare capacity violations, and economic losses. We conduct a rigorous sensitivity analysis of the prediction horizon N, demonstrating that the closed loop is robust to the horizon choice and that N = 35 days minimizes the realized cost. Furthermore, both the closed-loop solution and an open-loop turnpike analysis across diverse initial conditions reveal that the celebrated "Hammer and Dance" mitigation strategy emerges naturally as the mathematical optimum: the optimal trajectories anchor to a unique suppression turnpike (maximum lockdown) to drive hospitalizations toward the disease-free equilibrium before progressively reopening the economy. Through a turnpike-based argument we establish practical asymptotic stability of the optimal operating point, providing a mathematically grounded decision-support tool for pandemic policy.

</details>


### [234] [Learning to control switching nonlinear systems with Koopman operator regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11344)
*Edoardo Caldarelli,Oleksii Kachaiev,Cesare Molinari,Lorenzo Rosasco*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对有限动作空间的非线性系统，利用再生核希尔伯特空间中的Koopman算子回归从有限样本中学习未知动力学，得到由控制变量切换的线性切换预测模型，并采用模型预测控制求解无限时域最优控制问题，从理论上推导了学习速率和MPC策略的次优性，最后通过Duffing振子数值仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有限动作空间非线性系统的辨识与控制问题，提供从有限样本中学习动力学并实现闭环控制的理论保证。

Method: 采用Koopman算子回归在RKHS中学习非线性动力学，得到线性切换预测模型；利用模型预测控制求解无限时域时变代价最优控制问题。

Result: 推导了Koopman动力学逼近的学习速率；在精确Koopman动力学和学习动力学两种情形下，量化了MPC策略的次优性；Duffing振子数值仿真验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该框架为有限动作空间非线性系统的数据驱动控制提供了理论保证和有效方法。

Abstract: In this work, we consider the identification and control of nonlinear systems with finite action spaces. The unknown dynamics are estimated from finite samples with Koopman operator regression in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space, yielding a linear switching predictive model, the switches governed by the value of the control variable. In order to perform control in closed-loop, the learned dynamics are employed in an infinite-horizon optimal control problem with time-varying stage cost, which is solved by means of model predictive control. In a theoretical analysis, we derive learning rates for the Koopman dynamics approximation. We further quantify, under suitable assumptions, the sub-optimality of the model predictive control strategy, both in the case of exact Koopman dynamics, and in the case of learned ones. Numerical simulations on the Duffing oscillator complement our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [235] [Inter-Stop Energy Prediction and Causal Driver Quantification for Dual-Source Trolleybuses via a Time-Aware Tabular Deep Learning Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11349)
*Wentao Zeng,Zijian Huang,Yiming Bie,Jiabin Wu,Jun Gong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时间感知的表格深度学习框架，用于无轨电车区间能耗管理，集成了周期性时间编码、参数高效批量集成骨干网络和贝叶斯超参数优化，并在因果解释管道中结合特征归因、线性非高斯无环模型和元学习器，在苏黎世数据集上实现MAPE 6.52%、R 0.982，发现再生制动比和平均速度是最强节能因子，滑行距离是主要耗能因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双源无轨电车的能耗模式受路线属性、高频轨迹和逐时天气等多源异构输入驱动，现有模型难以表征这些输入且很少解释能耗的因果驱动因素。

Method: 提出时间感知表格深度学习框架，集成周期性时间编码与参数高效批量集成骨干网络以联合学习静态和序列特征，采用贝叶斯优化与树结构密度估计进行超参数调优；构建三层因果解释管道：特征归因分析边际效应、线性非高斯无环模型发现因果方向、元学习器估计净平均处理效应。

Result: 在苏黎世无轨电车数据集上达到MAPE 6.52%、R 0.982，超越十种统计、树集成和深度学习基线，消融实验表明周期性时间编码贡献最大；因果分析识别再生制动比和平均速度为最强节能因子，滑行距离为主要耗能驱动因素。

Conclusion: 本方法提供了可操作的阈值，用于指导车辆技术、驾驶行为、容量分配和接触网网络规划。

Abstract: Dual-source trolleybuses alternate between overhead catenary supply and on-board battery operation, creating energy-use patterns driven by route attributes, high-frequency trajectories, and hourly weather. Existing models struggle to represent these heterogeneous inputs and rarely explain the causal drivers of consumption. This paper proposes a time-aware tabular deep learning framework for inter-stop energy management. Periodic time encoding is integrated into a parameter-efficient batch-ensemble backbone to jointly learn static and sequential features, while Bayesian optimization with tree-structured density estimation tunes hyperparameters. To move beyond prediction, a three-layer causal explanation pipeline combines feature attribution for marginal effects, a linear non-Gaussian acyclic model for causal direction discovery, and a meta-learner for net average treatment effects. Experiments on the Zurich trolleybus dataset enriched with meteorological records achieve a MAPE of 6.52% and R of 0.982, outperforming ten statistical, tree-ensemble, and deep learning baselines. Ablation results show that periodic time encoding contributes most to the accuracy gain. Causal analysis identifies regenerative braking ratio and average speed as the strongest energy-saving factors, while coasting distance is the main driver of excess consumption. The findings offer actionable thresholds for vehicle technology, driving behavior, capacity allocation, and catenary network planning.

</details>


### [236] [Robust sensor coverage in the presence of spatial obstacles and exclusion zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11449)
*Vanshika Datta,C. Nahak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对位置不确定性下障碍物感知的空中定向传感器网络，提出了一个鲁棒优化框架，包含三种渐进式优化策略（RAGC、RATC、RATS），通过基于鲁棒可行半径的定向优化和目标感知的休眠调度机制，在传感器扰动下最大化有效网格和目标覆盖，并在多种仿真场景下显著优于现有代表性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中定向传感器网络在位置不确定性和障碍物遮挡条件下，缺乏统一的几何建模和鲁棒优化框架来保证感知性能。

Method: 将监控区域离散化为网格点，建立统一几何公式化模型描述定向感知并显式处理障碍物引起的可见性损失和排除区域；提出三种渐进式优化策略：RAGC（鲁棒空中网格覆盖）、RATC（鲁棒空中目标覆盖）和RATS（鲁棒空中目标调度）；采用基于鲁棒可行半径的定向优化最大化有效覆盖，并结合目标感知的休眠调度机制最小化活动传感器数量。

Result: 大量仿真表明，该框架在多种目标分布、障碍物配置、不确定性水平和传感器故障场景下，在覆盖质量、目标监控和传感器利用率方面始终优于代表性对比方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒优化框架能够实现鲁棒且节能的感知，有效应对位置不确定性和障碍物遮挡挑战。

Abstract: In this article, we develop a robust optimization framework for obstacle-aware aerial directional sensor networks operating under positional uncertainty using a sector-based sensing model. The monitoring area is discretized into grid points and a unified geometric formulation is developed to model directional sensing while explicitly accounting for obstacle-induced visibility loss and exclusion regions. To enhance sensing performance under uncertainty, three progressive optimization strategies, namely robust aerial grid coverage (RAGC), robust aerial target coverage (RATC) and robust aerial target scheduling (RATS), are proposed. The framework employs robust orientation optimization based on the radius of robust feasibility to maximize effective grid and target coverage under sensor perturbations, followed by a target-aware sleep scheduling mechanism that minimizes the number of active sensors without compromising target monitoring. Extensive simulations under varying target distributions, obstacle configurations, uncertainty levels and sensor failure scenarios demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves robust and energy-efficient sensing while consistently outperforming representative approaches from the literature in terms of coverage quality, target monitoring and sensor utilization.

</details>


### [237] [First-Order Methods for Distributionally Robust Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11460)
*Hubert Villuendas,Mathieu Besançon,Jérôme Malick*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合熵正则化和随机Frank-Wolfe算法的可扩展方法，用于求解带约束的Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化问题，并在最小二次生成树和交通分配问题上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际优化问题中的输入数据常受估计误差影响，Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化虽能缓解模型风险，但求解带约束的组合优化问题在数值上仍然困难。

Method: (i) 对分布鲁棒值函数进行熵正则化，使其可计算随机梯度估计量；(ii) 将随机梯度估计量与随机Frank-Wolfe算法结合，在优化正则化鲁棒目标的同时自然处理约束。

Result: 在最小二次生成树和交通分配两个经典问题上验证了方法的有效性，并展示了相对于经验风险最小化的优势。

Conclusion: 该方法为处理带约束的Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化问题提供了一种通用且实用的途径。

Abstract: We consider constrained optimization problems in which input data are affected by estimation errors. In such settings, Wasserstein distributionally robust optimization provides a principled framework to mitigate model risk by optimizing against worst-case distributions within Wasserstein ambiguity sets. However, the numerical resolution of the resulting problems remains challenging, especially in constrained and combinatorial settings. In this paper, we propose a tractable stochastic approach based on two key ingredients: (i) an entropic regularization of the distributionally robust value function, which makes it possible to compute stochastic gradient estimators, and (ii) the combination of these estimators with a stochastic Frank-Wolfe algorithm, allowing us to optimize the regularized robust objective while naturally handling constraints. We illustrate the method, and its interests against empirical risk minimization, on two classical optimization problems, the minimum quadratic spanning tree and the traffic assignment problems. Our approach provides a general, practical way to address Wasserstein distributionally robust formulations in the presence of constraints.

</details>


### [238] [Copositive Characterizations of Convex Hull Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11590)
*Madhusudan Ghosh,Antoine Lesage-Landry,Joshua Adam Taylor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了机组组合问题的凸包定价与边缘copositive对偶价格在非退化条件下等价，并通过Scarf算例验证了该等价性及半定松弛引入的定价间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机组组合问题的非凸二元约束导致不存在统一的线性定价方案，凸包定价和copositive对偶定价均试图解决该问题，但二者关系尚不明确，需理论统一。

Method: 定义联合可行集上的集中式凸包价格，通过非退化条件证明其与边缘copositive对偶价格一致，并利用Scarf算例进行数值验证。

Result: 证明了凸包价格与边缘copositive对偶价格在非退化条件下等价；通过Scarf算例量化了半定松弛引入的定价间隙。

Conclusion: 凸包定价与copositive对偶定价存在理论等价关系，半定松弛虽可近似但会引入定价偏差。

Abstract: Due to the nonconvex binary constraints of unit commitment (UC), no uniform linear pricing scheme supports the optimal dispatch. Convex hull pricing (CHP) and copositive duality pricing (CDP) both address this problem. CHP derives the price from the subgradient of the value function of the convex hull relaxation of UC. CDP refers to several different pricing mechanisms that can be constructed from the dual multipliers of the completely positive programming reformulation. In this work, we define a centralized convex hull price over the joint feasible set of UC and prove that, under non-degeneracy, it coincides with the marginal copositive duality price. Numerical experiments on the Scarf example validate this equivalence and quantify the pricing gap introduced by the semidefinite restriction.

</details>


### [239] [Scenario Reduction for Two-Stage Stochastic Mixed-Integer Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11593)
*Yannick Werner,Juan Miguel Morales,Salvador Pineda,Line Roald,Sonja Wogrin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对两阶段随机混合整数规划中的场景缩减问题，提出了一种新的运输成本函数及混合算法，在仅使用约5个场景的情况下将近似误差控制在0.4%-2.1%以内，并在大规模算例中将计算时间降低18倍、求解器工作量降低66倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段随机混合整数规划中，用大量场景表示不确定性虽能保证结果准确，但导致求解困难。场景缩减旨在用更少场景逼近原始分布的最优第一阶段决策，现有成本函数存在改进空间。

Method: 基于概率分布距离与最优质量运输问题重访经典场景缩减理论；提出新的运输成本函数；采用Forward Selection算法证明该函数在首次抽取时能选出相对近似误差最优的场景；为降低计算开销，进一步提出包含场景预选阶段的混合算法。

Result: 在24节点和300节点的两阶段随机机组组合问题中，仅约5个场景下新成本函数分别达到2.1%和0.4%的近似误差；混合算法在大规模算例中墙钟时间降低18倍，Gurobi求解器工作量降低66倍，同时保持相近的求解质量。

Conclusion: 提出的新运输成本函数和混合算法能高效实现场景缩减，在极小场景数下达到高精度近似，并大幅降低计算成本，适用于大规模随机规划问题。

Abstract: Two-stage stochastic mixed-integer programs (MIPs) are important tools for decision-making under uncertainty, but representing uncertainty with a large number of scenarios -- while ensuring accurate results -- can make them challenging to solve. Scenario reduction addresses this by finding a distribution supported on fewer scenarios that still yields similar optimal first-stage decisions. In this paper, we revisit the classical scenario reduction theory based on distances between probability distributions and the optimal mass transportation problem. We then review and compare various transportation cost functions from the literature and propose a new one. Using the Forward Selection Algorithm, we prove that our proposed cost function selects the best possible scenario from a given sample on the first draw with respect to the relative approximation error. To reduce the computational cost of evaluating this cost function, we further propose a hybrid algorithm that includes a scenario pre-selection phase. We assess solution quality and computational complexity on the two-stage stochastic unit commitment problem for small 24-bus and large 300-bus case studies. With only around five scenarios, the proposed cost function approximates the full-distribution optimum to within roughly 2.1% and 0.4% error for the small and large cases, respectively. The hybrid algorithm achieves similar solution quality while reducing wall-clock time by a factor of 18 and work (as measured by the Gurobi solver) by a factor of 66 on the large case study.

</details>


### [240] [On the Policy Convergence of Policy Mirror Descent Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11626)
*Wenye Li,Ke Wei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了未正则化的策略镜像下降（PMD）算法在有限折扣MDP中的策略收敛性，证明在一般可分解镜像映射和任意常数步长下，策略序列收敛到最优策略，即使最优策略集非单点集。分析揭示了收敛行为由ψ在0和1处的可微性决定，包括有限时间终止、渐近收敛和MDP依赖的二分法，并在ψ二次连续可微时建立了局部收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管策略收敛性已在特定PMD实例或正则化变体（如同伦PMD）中得到证明，但尚未有对未正则化PMD在一般可分解镜像映射和任意常数步长下的系统性和统一性策略收敛理论。

Method: 研究有限折扣MDP中未正则化的策略镜像下降（PMD）算法，采用可分解镜像映射形式h(p)=∑_a ψ(p(a))，其中ψ满足标准Legendre型假设，分析在不同可微性条件下的收敛行为。

Result: 证明了PMD生成的策略序列收敛到极限最优策略；收敛行为由ψ在0和1处的可微性决定，呈现有限时间终止、渐近收敛和MDP依赖的二分法；当ψ二次连续可微且具有严格正有限曲率时，建立了局部策略收敛速率。

Conclusion: 这是首个未正则化PMD在一般可分解镜像映射和任意常数步长下的统一策略收敛理论，覆盖了平方欧几里得镜像映射、负香农熵、Tsallis熵、Hellinger映射和Fermi-Dirac熵等多种常用镜像映射。

Abstract: We study the policy convergence of unregularized policy mirror descent (PMD) with arbitrary constant step sizes for finite discounted Markov decision processes. We focus on decomposable mirror maps of the form $h(p)=\sum_a ψ(p(a))$, where $ψ$ satisfies standard Legendre-type assumptions. Under these conditions, we prove that the policy sequence generated by PMD converges in the policy domain to a limiting optimal policy, even when the optimal policy set is not a singleton. This result covers a broad class of commonly used mirror maps, including the squared Euclidean mirror map underlying projected Q-ascent, the negative Shannon entropy underlying softmax natural policy gradient, Tsallis entropy, the Hellinger mapping, and the Fermi-Dirac entropy. Although policy convergence has been established previously for specific PMD instances or for regularized variants such as homotopic PMD, to the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic and unified policy convergence theory for unregularized PMD under general decomposable mirror maps and arbitrary constant step sizes. Our analysis further reveals that the convergence behavior is governed by the differentiability of $ψ$ at $0$ and $1$, leading to different behaviors, including finite-time termination, asymptotic convergence, and an MDP-dependent dichotomy. When $ψ$ is twice continuously differentiable with strictly positive finite curvature, we further establish local policy convergence rates for the asymptotic convergence cases, covering the standard mirror maps mentioned above.

</details>


### [241] [Lecture Notes: Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11664)
*Andreas Habring*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 2026年格拉茨技术大学Andreas Habring主讲的凸优化课程讲义，涵盖数学基础、最小化问题解的存在性与基本结果、投影次梯度下降法、近端梯度法、重球梯度下降、Nesterov加速梯度下降与FISTA、原始-对偶方法，以及最优传输简介。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为凸优化课程提供系统性的教学材料，使学生掌握从基础理论到高级算法的完整知识体系，并引入最优传输等前沿应用方向。

Method: 课程采用渐进式组织方式：先建立数学基础（凸分析、次梯度等），再依次讲解各类优化算法（投影次梯度下降、近端梯度、重球法、Nesterov加速法/FISTA、原始-对偶方法），最后介绍最优传输这一应用领域。

Result: 形成了一套覆盖凸优化核心理论与算法的综合课程讲义，内容从基础概念延伸到前沿应用。

Conclusion: 该讲义系统梳理了凸优化领域的核心内容，既包含经典算法（如投影次梯度下降、重球法），也涵盖现代加速技术（Nesterov/FISTA）和前沿方向（最优传输），适用于研究生层次的凸优化课程教学。

Abstract: Lecture notes for a course on convex optimization taught by Andreas Habring at Graz University of Technology in 2026. The notes cover mathematical preliminaries, fundamental results about existence and of solutions for minimization problems, projected subgradient descent, proximal-gradient methods, heavy ball gradient descent, Nesterov accelerated gradient descent and FISTA, primal-dual methods, and a short intro to optimal transport.

</details>


### [242] [Contextual Stochastic Optimization with Decision-Dependent Uncertainty via Nonparametric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11714)
*Huangrong Sun,Xian Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类决策依赖的情境随机规划问题，其中不确定性同时依赖于外生上下文信息和内生决策。采用kNN、CART和ReLU神经网络三种非参数回归模型学习不确定性依赖关系，并提出基于经验残差的决策依赖样本平均近似（ER-DD-SAA）框架。针对每种非参数模型开发了精确混合整数规划表示，并为两阶段kNN问题设计了BD-CG分解算法。理论上证明了算法的有限收敛性及统计一致性和渐近最优性。数值实验表明该方法在样本外表现和计算效率上均优于参数基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机规划通常假设不确定性独立于决策或仅依赖于有限参数化形式，但实际中不确定性可能同时受决策和上下文信息影响且依赖关系复杂。现有方法难以有效建模这种复杂依赖关系，且缺乏兼具统计一致性和计算可处理性的框架。

Method: (1) 采用kNN、CART、ReLU神经网络三种非参数回归模型学习不确定性对决策和上下文信息的依赖； (2) 提出ER-DD-SAA框架，通过添加经验残差校正点预测误差； (3) 为每种非参数模型构建嵌入ER-DD-SAA的精确MIP表示； (4) 针对两阶段kNN问题设计结合Benders分解与约束生成的BD-CG算法； (5) 从统计角度建立三种模型的一致性及渐近最优性理论。

Result: (1) 证明了BD-CG算法在有限迭代次数内收敛到全局最优； (2) 证明了ER-DD-SAA在温和正则性条件下具有统计一致性和渐近最优性； (3) 数值实验表明：在报童定价问题和两阶段设施选址问题中，ER-DD-SAA模型样本外表现一致优于参数基准方法，且所提出的重构和算法显著提升了计算可处理性。

Conclusion: 该工作为决策依赖不确定性下的随机规划提供了一套完整的非参数学习-优化框架，同时保证了统计性质和计算可处理性，为实际复杂决策问题提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We study a general decision-dependent contextual stochastic program (DD-CSP) in which uncertainty depends on both exogenous contextual information and endogenous decisions. To learn the potentially complex dependence of uncertainty on decisions and contextual information, we employ several nonparametric regression models, including k nearest neighbors (kNN), classification and regression trees (CART), and ReLU neural networks. To account for estimation errors in predicting the uncertainty, we adopt an empirical residuals-based decision-dependent sample average approximation (ER-DD-SAA) framework, which adds empirical residuals to the point predictions from the learned regression models. For each nonparametric regression model, we develop exact mixed-integer programming (MIP) representations that can be seamlessly embedded within the ER-DD-SAA framework. For two-stage ER-DD-SAA problems with kNN, we further propose a tailored decomposition algorithm, named BD-CG, that combines Bender's decomposition with constraint generation. Under suitable assumptions, we prove that the proposed BD-CG converges to a global optimum within a finite number of iterations. From a statistical perspective, we establish the consistency and asymptotic optimality of ER-DD-SAA with all three nonparametric regression models under mild regularity conditions. Numerical experiments on a newsvendor problem with pricing and a two-stage facility location problem demonstrate that the ER-DD-SAA model with nonparametric learning consistently outperforms a parametric benchmark in out-of-sample performance and the proposed reformulations and algorithm substantially improve computational tractability.

</details>


### [243] [Sparse Robust Optimal Control in Continuous-Time: A Computationally Viable Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11827)
*Siddhartha Ganguly,Ashwin Aravind,Souvik Das,Masaaki Nagahara,Debasish Chatterjee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的数值可行算法，用于求解连续时间下的稀疏鲁棒最优控制问题。通过将问题转化为半无限规划(SIP)形式，并进一步转化为有限维凸优化问题，首次实现了稀疏最优控制中精确解的计算，同时保证了不可数约束的满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏最优控制问题在连续时间下缺乏能够计算精确解的数值可行算法，现有方法通常只能获得近似解，亟需一种既能保证解的最优性又能满足无穷约束的高效求解框架。

Method: 1) 将带L1型目标函数的约束线性噪声ODE系统的最优控制问题转化为半无限规划(SIP)形式；2) 在该SIP框架基础上，构建一个有限维且计算可行的凸优化问题，以无损方式恢复原始SIP的最优值和对应优化器；3) 证明了参数依赖的噪声系统和最小注意力问题均可纳入该框架。

Result: 首次在稀疏最优控制领域实现了精确解的计算，所提出的有限维凸优化问题能够无损恢复原始SIP的最优值和优化器，并保证不可数约束的满足。通过基准数值示例验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该算法为连续时间稀疏鲁棒最优控制问题提供了一种全新的精确求解框架，且可推广至参数依赖噪声系统和最小注意力问题，具有重要的理论价值和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This article presents a novel, numerically viable algorithm for solving sparse robust optimal control problems in continuous time. We consider a constrained linear noisy system governed by an ordinary differential equation (ODE), with an $L^1$-type objective function in line with the sparse optimal control literature. The resulting optimal control problem is shown to admit a semi-infinite programming (SIP) formulation. Building upon this insight, we develop a new framework that enables the computation of exact solutions -- to our knowledge, the first such achievement in the context of sparse optimal control. We demonstrate that a finite and computationally viable convex optimization problem can be solved to recover, in a lossless manner, both the optimal value and the corresponding optimizers of the original SIP, while also guaranteeing satisfaction of uncountably many constraints. We also show that the parameter-dependent noisy systems and the minimum attention problem fall into our framework and can be solved efficiently via our algorithm. The efficacy of our algorithm is illustrated through a benchmark numerical example.

</details>


### [244] [Stabilize-then-optimize: Feedback transformations as preconditioners in optimal control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11835)
*Till Preuster,Manuel Schaller,Anton Schiela,Martin Stoll*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出通过反馈变换重新表述最优控制问题，以降低控制-状态映射的范数，从而显著改善相关条件数，并针对常微分方程和各类偏微分方程（抛物型、双曲型、椭圆型）给出了构造性方法及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多最优控制数值算法（如凝聚方法、最优性系统的预条件共轭梯度法）依赖于通过控制-状态映射消除状态变量，该映射的范数直接影响收敛估计（如线性系统的条件数）。为了改善条件数、提升算法收敛性，需要降低控制-状态映射的范数。

Method: 利用反馈变换重新表述最优控制问题，通过反馈降低（反馈后的）控制-状态映射的范数，从而改善相关条件数。针对常微分方程以及抛物型、双曲型、椭圆型偏微分方程分别提供了构造性方法。

Result: 反馈变换能显著降低控制-状态映射的范数，大幅改善条件数；通过椭圆型、抛物型和双曲型偏微分方程的数值算例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 反馈变换是一种通用的、有效的手段，可用于改进各类最优控制问题中控制-状态映射的范数及其相关条件数，从而提升数值算法的收敛性能。

Abstract: Many numerical algorithms for optimal control leverage an elimination of the state via the control-to-state map such as condensed approaches or preconditioned conjugate gradient methods for the optimality system. As such, the norm of the control-to-state map directly enters the convergence estimates for these methods, e.g., via the condition number of the associated linear system. In this work we show that using feedback transformations one may reformulate the optimal control problem to decrease the norm of the (feedbacked) control-to-state map, leading to a drastic improvement of the involved condition numbers. We illustrate the abstract approach for ordinary and partial differential equations such as parabolic, hyperbolic or elliptic equations. For each of these problem classes we provide a constructive method to improve solution operator norms via feedbacks. Further, we showcase the efficacy of the method by means of various numerical examples with elliptic, parabolic and hyperbolic partial differential equations.

</details>


### [245] [Optimal Parameter-Free First-Order Methods for Convex Optimization with Unknown Growth and Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11878)
*Liwei Jiang,Ke Tang,Zhe Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需先验知识的确定性一阶凸优化方法（Bundle-Level W-Certificate, BLW 及其加速变体 A-BLW），能够自适应目标函数的增长特性、光滑性及其参数，在非光滑、弱光滑和光滑场景下均达到最优复杂度。核心创新在于一种基于仿射下降缓慢性的W-证书条件，将束模型几何转化为二次增长下的最优性差距保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凸优化一阶方法通常需要预先知道目标函数的增长模量、光滑性参数或目标精度等先验知识才能达到最优复杂度，缺乏能够在未知这些信息的情况下自适应地达到最优性能的方法。

Method: 提出Bundle-Level W-Certificate (BLW)方法及其加速变体A-BLW，核心是定义一个基于仿射下降缓慢性的W-证书条件，该条件将束模型的几何信息转化为二次增长假设下的最优性间隙保证。通过停时分析，算法无需任何参数作为输入即可自适应达到最优复杂度。

Result: 在非光滑Lipschitz且满足二次增长的目标上，BLW达到最优复杂度；A-BLW在非光滑、弱光滑和光滑三种场景下均达到最优速率，且无需知道Hölder光滑性参数、二次增长模量或目标精度。A-BLW无需修改即可在一般凸目标和满足至少二阶Hölder增长的目标上达到已知最优速率。

Conclusion: 提出的方法首次实现了在完全无先验知识条件下自适应达到凸优化各场景下的最优复杂度，数值实验验证了实际有效性。

Abstract: We study deterministic first-order minimization of a convex function without prior knowledge of the objective's growth, smoothness regime, or associated parameters. We develop anytime, parameter-free bundle-level methods that adapt simultaneously to these unknown properties and attain best-known oracle complexities. For nonsmooth Lipschitz objectives satisfying quadratic growth, the proposed bundle-level W-certificate method (BLW) achieves the optimal complexity without requiring the growth modulus or target accuracy as input. We then introduce an accelerated variant, A-BLW. Without knowing the Hölder smoothness parameters, the quadratic-growth modulus, or the target accuracy, A-BLW attains the optimal rates in the nonsmooth, weakly smooth, and smooth regimes. Central to both methods is an affine W-certificate, a condition based on the descent-slowness of an affine minorant that converts the geometry of a bundle model into an optimality-gap guarantee under quadratic growth. A stopping-time analysis further shows that the same A-BLW algorithm, without modification, achieves the corresponding best-known rates for general convex objectives and for objectives satisfying Hölder growth of order at least two. Numerical experiments illustrate the practical performance of the proposed methods.

</details>

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm in Knowledge Graphs (KGs) due to their intrinsic ability to model graph-structured data. However, there remains a lack of a systematic review about GNN-based methodologies across the entire knowledge graph technologies pipeline. To address this gap, we first propose a novel two-level taxonomy framework for GNN-based knowledge graph technologies: the KG technologies pipeline and GNN-based perspective. Specifically, the knowledge graph technologies pipeline covers knowledge graph construction, knowledge graph embedding, knowledge reasoning and knowledge graph applications. Meanwhile, the GNN-based perspective provides a new categorization of knowledge graph technologies with GNN models, such as GCN, GAT, and HGNN. Then, we analyze the advantages of GNN technology based on the characteristics of different tasks in the knowledge graph lifecycle. Furthermore, we detailed review various GNN-based models for knowledge graph following the proposed taxonomy, and summarize strengths and limitations. Finally, we discuss unresolved challenges and outline promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [247] [Position: Every Ground Truth is a Human Construction, not an Objective Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09668)
*Charlotte Högberg,Ericka Johnson,Kiri L. Wagstaff*

Abstract: Ground truth datasets play a fundamental role as reference values in the training and evaluation of machine learning models. This position paper argues that ground truths are not neutral objective measurements that are naturally given, but instead that they are constructed by arrangements of humans and technologies. We argue that the ML community will benefit from articulating and discussing these often invisible or unreported choices and acknowledging that reference data sets are contingent, not universal. Focusing on the situated and context-dependent nature of ground truths can improve reliability by enabling a better informed perspective on where, when, and how the datasets, and the models they have shaped, can best be used. We argue for increasing `situated reliability' which includes articulating the limits and strengths of models and their truth claims. Finally, paying more attention to the construction of ground truths can support transparency, accountability, and interdisciplinary work.

</details>


### [248] [AuditWeave: A Tamper-Evident, Auditor-Navigable Evidence Layer for AI-Assisted and Data-Transformation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09682)
*Vimal Nakrani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly used to assist consequential decisions in regulated domains such as auditing, finance, and healthcare. This creates a recurring obligation: an organization must be able to reconstruct, after the fact, which evidence informed a given conclusion, and to show that the record of that reasoning was not altered. Existing tools address related but distinct problems - model observability, drift monitoring, governance reporting - and are built for the machine-learning engineer operating a system, not the reviewer who must trace one specific conclusion back to its supporting evidence. We present AuditWeave, a lightweight Python library, with no runtime dependencies, that records the steps of AI-assisted and data-transformation workflows into a single append-only, hash-chained ledger. A small, system-agnostic event vocabulary spans both retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines and tabular/lakehouse transformations, so a conclusion that draws on both can be traced end-to-end through one record. Within a sealed ledger, any modification, reordering, insertion, or deletion of events is detectable through chain verification. We describe the design and evaluate recording overhead, scalability, and tamper-detection correctness on the reference implementation. The integrity guarantees cost tens of microseconds per event, and, as the hash-chain construction implies, verification flagged every injected mutation across four mutation classes over 2,000 randomized trials.

</details>

### [250] [SciML in the Wild: A Diagnostic Study of When Structural Priors Help and When They Hurt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09684)
*Vrishank Sai Anand,Prathamesh Dinesh Joshi,Raj Abhijit Dandekar,Rajat Dandekar,Sreedath Panat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) methods such as Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (NODEs), Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), and Universal Differential Equations (UDEs) are most effective when structural priors reflect reliable governing dynamics. We ask what happens when this assumption is violated. Using macroeconomic forecasting as a stress-test domain, we evaluate five model families, ARIMA, LSTM, NODE, PINN, and UDE, across 23 countries using sparse annual data, multiple temporal splits, and five random seeds. Our results show that none of the evaluated models achieve consistently strong forecasting performance, highlighting the difficulty of low-frequency macroeconomic prediction. However, a clear relative hierarchy emerges: less-constrained models, particularly ARIMA and NODE, consistently outperform more-constrained heuristic-prior models such as PINN and UDE. Rather than treating this as a rejection of SciML, we interpret it as a diagnostic result: structural priors can act as misregularizers when they do not match the data-generating process. We identify failure modes including prior misalignment, regime shifts, structural breaks, and optimization instability, and argue that SciML practitioners should test whether structure helps before assuming that more structure is beneficial.

</details>


### [251] [MawForge: Memory-Bounded Expert Materialization for Local Mixture-of-Experts Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09686)
*Craig Opie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MawForge提出了一种在统一内存设备上本地运行MoE模型的新方法：将完整模型存储在磁盘，常驻张量保留在内存，按需将路由专家张量加载到有限执行缓存中。研究表明该方法作为有限执行机制和测量平台有效，但作为缓存最大化策略效果不佳，性能取决于专家复用、驻留空间、KV缓存大小、量化、路由局部性和系统内存压力之间的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型总参数量远超每token活跃计算量，但本地推理系统仍需要完整模型、KV缓存、运行时缓冲区及操作系统预留空间全部放入快速内存中，这使得资源受限的统一内存机器上部署MoE模型变得困难。MawForge旨在探索一种不同的系统假设，使本地MoE服务在资源受限设备上变得可行。

Method: 将完整模型存储在磁盘上，仅将常驻张量（如共享参数）保留在内存中，当推理需要时，按需将路由专家张量从磁盘加载到有界执行缓存中，缓存大小受限于可用内存预算。

Result: MawForge作为有限执行机制和测量平台有效，但作为缓存最大化策略效果不佳。整体性能取决于专家重用率、驻留内存占用、KV缓存大小、量化精度、路由局部性以及macOS系统内存压力等多个因素的平衡。

Conclusion: 本地MoE推理在统一内存机器上的实践依赖于对多个系统因素的精细平衡，简单的缓存最大化策略并非最优解，需要综合考虑专家复用模式、内存预算分配和操作系统级内存压力管理。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models separate total parameter count from per-token active computation, but local inference systems often still require the full model, key-value cache, runtime buffers, and operatingsystem headroom to fit in fast memory. MawForge tests a different systems hypothesis: local MoE serving can be made practical on constrained unified-memory machines by storing the full model on disk, keeping common tensors resident, and materializing routed expert tensors into a bounded execution cache on demand. The central finding is that MawForge is effective as a bounded execution mechanism and measurement substrate for local MoE inference, but not as a cache-maximization policy. Performance depends on balancing expert reuse against resident footprint, KV-cache size, quantization, route locality, and macOS memory pressure.

</details>


### [252] [Prioritizing Search Space Regions in the Low Autocorrelation Binary Sequences Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09688)
*Blaž Pšeničnik,Borko Bošković,Jan Popić,Janez Brest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合Thompson采样与并行自避行走的混合搜索框架，通过在LABS问题的限制类间自适应分配计算资源，实现了对35个序列长度（450≤L≤527）及L=573的先前最优结果的改进，并首次在L=451处获得超过8.0的优值因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低自相关二值序列问题（LABS）是通信、信号处理和卫星导航等领域的重要组合优化难题，现有方法在处理长序列时面临搜索空间过大、计算资源分配效率低下的挑战。

Method: 提出混合搜索框架：1) 将LABS搜索空间的分区建模为多臂老虎机中的臂，利用Thompson采样动态分配计算资源；2) 结合并行自避行走在选定分区内高效搜索；3) 采用GPU并行执行、共享后验更新、高效邻域评估和布隆过滤器防环；4) 两阶段优化策略：先搜索约束分区斜对称空间，再在无约束空间中精炼最佳候选解。

Result: 在35个序列长度（450≤L≤527）和L=573上改进了先前最优结果，在L=451处首次获得优值因子超过8.0的新最长序列。Thompson采样有效优先选择性能更优的分区，验证了在线数据驱动资源分配的价值。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可扩展且有效的优值因子最大化策略，通过自适应资源分配和混合搜索机制在高维LABS问题上取得了显著改进。

Abstract: Low autocorrelation binary sequences problem (LABS) is a hard combinatorial optimization challenge with important applications in communications, signal processing, and satellite navigation. This paper proposes a hybrid search framework that combines Thompson sampling with parallel self-avoiding walks to adaptively allocate computational effort across restriction classes of the LABS search space. By modeling partitions as arms in a multi-armed bandit setting, the proposed method dynamically shifts search resources toward partitions that empirically produce higher merit factors while maintaining exploration of less-sampled regions. The approach is further accelerated through GPU-parallel execution, shared posterior updates, efficient neighborhood evaluation, and a Bloom filter for cycle prevention. In addition, we use a two-stage optimization strategy that first searches constrained partitioned skew-symmetric spaces and then refines the best candidates in the unrestricted space. Experiments on long binary sequences show that the proposed method improves the previously best-known results for 35 sequence lengths in the range $450 \le L \le 527$ and for $L=573$. In particular, we report a new longest sequence with merit factor exceeding $8.0$, obtained for $L=451$. The results also show that Thompson sampling effectively prioritizes partitions with better observed performance, confirming the value of online, data-driven resource allocation in LABS optimization. Overall, the proposed framework provides a scalable and effective strategy for high-performance merit factor maximization.

</details>


### [253] [What Context Does a Coding Agent Actually Need to Act?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09691)
*Brian Sam-Bodden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究编码智能体在编辑代码时真正需要哪些上下文信息，发现关键信号仅存在于被编辑的代码本身，自然语言摘要和周围上下文几乎不起作用，压缩上下文可以匹配完整文件效果但仅用三分之一tokens。同时发现温度0的API推理会导致约9%的结果波动，成为该基准测试的噪声基底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编码智能体可以容纳整个仓库的上下文，但大部分阅读是浪费的。作者希望探究智能体在编辑代码时真正需要什么信息，而不是能使用多少上下文。

Method: 将"定位编辑位置"与"执行编辑操作"分离，使用oracle固定定位，仅改变代码表示方式。对比自然语言摘要与源代码、移除周围上下文和使用UML骨架及签名、压缩上下文等不同表示，在SWE-bench Verified基准上评估实际问题解决效果。

Result: 1) 自然语言摘要几乎无法回答源码能回答的行为问题（4/45 vs 27/45），且前沿模型摘要与3B模型同样差；2) 将文件剩余部分替换为UML骨架和签名与直接删除无显著差异（p=0.75），假设未成立；3) 压缩上下文匹配完整文件效果，但每解决一个问题仅需19K tokens而非94K；4) 温度0的API推理在字节相同的运行间翻转约9%的结果。

Conclusion: 编码智能体编辑代码时真正需要的是被编辑代码本身的源码表示，自然语言摘要和周围上下文贡献有限。压缩上下文是更高效的表示方式。此外，基准测试中存在约9%的噪声基底，需要关注。

Abstract: A modern coding agent can hold an entire repository in its context window. Most of its reading is wasted -- and the interesting question is not how much context an agent can use, but what it actually \emph{needs}. We study that question at the moment it matters most: when the agent must \emph{edit} code. Separating \emph{finding} the work site from \emph{acting} on it, we hold localization fixed with an oracle, vary only how the code is represented, and score context against real issue resolution on SWE-bench Verified. The answer is starkly minimal. The signal lives in the code being edited itself: natural-language summaries of it answer almost none of the behavioral questions that the source answers ($4/45$ vs.\ $27/45$, held-out repositories, independent judge), and the gap belongs to the representation, not the summarizer -- a frontier model's summaries score exactly as poorly as a 3B model's. The surrounding context hardly matters either: across every multi-file instance in Verified, under a protocol frozen before any data, rendering a file's remainder as UML skeletons and signatures resolves no more issues than deleting that remainder outright ($N{=}70$, exact McNemar $p{=}0.75$). That was our registered hypothesis, and it failed. Compressed context, meanwhile, matches whole files at a third of the tokens: a resolved issue costs $19$K context tokens, not $94$K. The instrument also yielded a finding the field should keep: temperature-0 API inference flips ${\sim}9\%$ of per-instance outcomes between byte-identical runs. That is a noise floor under every small effect reported on this benchmark, including ours. We release the instrument -- gold-validated environments, per-instance proof that every reference edit is expressible from every arm's context, deterministic patch construction, and pre-registered hypotheses whose nulls we publish.

</details>


### [254] [Reference-Based Distillation Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09692)
*Rajat Rawat,Sizhe Chen,Akshay Anand,Michael Duan,Bob Rotsted,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于参考的成员推断方法，通过比较学生模型对不同候选教师模型输出的偏好程度，来检测模型是否被蒸馏以及识别其教师模型。该方法在单教师蒸馏场景下近乎完美地恢复了真实教师，并成功应用于QwQ、DeepSeek-R1和GPT-OSS等当代模型的蒸馏关系分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型蒸馏广泛用于提升性能，但带来了不公平优势和违反政策的问题。因此，能否检测一个模型是否从另一个模型蒸馏而来成为一个基础性问题。

Method: 提出基于参考的成员推断方法：通过比较学生模型相对于参考检查点对不同候选教师输出的一致性偏好来识别最可能的教师。针对未知蒸馏流程（如隐藏提示），从模型输出中推断代理提示模板。还识别了o1/o3模型特有的字形级信号。

Result: 在受控蒸馏实验和真实模型上，该方法在单教师蒸馏场景下以近乎完美的准确率恢复真实教师。成功应用于QwQ、DeepSeek-R1和GPT-OSS等当代模型，揭示了潜在的蒸馏关系。

Conclusion: 检测模型蒸馏是可行的，尤其在参考设置下。该方法为理解现代模型之间的蒸馏关系提供了新证据，并可用于监测政策违规。

Abstract: Model distillation -- training on outputs from stronger third-party models -- is widely used to boost performance, but raises concerns about unfair advantages and policy violations. This motivates a fundamental question: can we detect whether a model was distilled from another? We show that, while identifying a teacher model from a student in isolation is highly challenging, it becomes tractable in a reference-based setting: given a model and an earlier-generation checkpoint from the same lineage, we can identify the teacher model used to train the later checkpoint. We introduce a distillation detection method based on reference-based membership inference. By comparing how strongly a student model preferentially aligns with outputs from different candidate teachers relative to a reference checkpoint, our method identifies the most likely teacher and detects evidence of distillation. To handle unknown distillation pipelines such as hidden prompts, we infer proxy prompt templates directly from model outputs. We additionally identify a distinctive glyph-level signal specific to o1/o3 models. Evaluating distillation detection is challenging because modern model lineages are already heavily entangled. To address this, we develop a hybrid evaluation spanning both controlled distillation experiments and real-world models. Across both settings, our approach recovers the true teacher with near-perfect accuracy in single-teacher distillation scenarios, even when the underlying distillation pipeline is largely unknown. We further introduce statistical tests for both teacher attribution and distillation detection, and extend our framework to open-world settings where no teacher is guaranteed to be present among the candidates. Applying our method to contemporary models yields new evidence regarding potential distillation relationships involving QwQ, DeepSeek-R1, and GPT-OSS.

</details>


### [255] [Depth-Entropy Guided Sampling for Training-Free LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09693)
*Zibin Meng,Peng Xie,Kani Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DEGS，一种无需训练、仅在测试时使用的采样方法，通过利用Transformer层间熵崩溃（layer-wise entropy collapse）作为内在质量信号，在推理基准上实现了与RL训练相媲美甚至超越的性能，且仅需少量计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习（RL）虽能提升LLM推理能力，但需要昂贵的训练、精心整理的数据和奖励信号；而测试时采样方法仅利用输出层似然，忽略了Transformer前向传播的内部动态。作者希望利用模型中隐含的内部信号（层间熵崩溃）来设计一种无需训练、低开销的测试时方法。

Method: 提出Depth-Entropy Guided Sampling (DEGS)：1) 观察强推理模型（包括RL后训练变体）呈现"晚期崩溃"现象——logit-lens解码熵在浅层保持高位，深层才收敛；2) 定义每个序列的崩溃深度 D(x)；3) 构建联合目标 π(x) ∝ p(x)^α exp(βD(x))，结合序列似然与深度熵结构；4) 在MCMC幂采样框架（DEGS-MCMC）中实例化。

Result: 在3个开源模型和4个推理基准上，DEGS达到了SOTA级无需训练的准确率，在域外和更困难的分支上增益最大，仅带来个位数百分比的墙钟开销。在数学分支上略低于GRPO，但在GPQA域外任务上全面超越GRPO（三个模型均如此）。

Conclusion: 层间熵崩溃可作为有效的内在质量信号，无需训练或奖励模型即可显著提升测试时采样质量，尤其在似然信号失效的域外和困难样本上表现突出。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become the dominant paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but it requires expensive training, curated data, and reward signals. Recent work shows that sampling from sharpened base-model distributions at test time recovers much of the RL gain, yet existing methods rely solely on output-layer likelihoods and ignore the transformer's internal forward-pass dynamics. We introduce Depth-Entropy Guided Sampling (DEGS), a training-free, test-time method that exploits layer-wise entropy collapse as an intrinsic quality signal. We observe that stronger reasoners -- including RL-posttrained variants -- exhibit a distinctive "late collapse": logit-lens decoded entropy stays elevated until deeper layers before converging. We define a per-sequence collapse depth $D(\mathbf{x})$ and a joint objective $π(\mathbf{x}) \propto p(\mathbf{x})^α\exp(βD(\mathbf{x}))$ that combines sequence likelihood with this depth-entropy structure, instantiated inside an MCMC power-sampling framework (DEGS-MCMC). Across three open-weight models and four reasoning benchmarks, this near-chance per-candidate signal compounds over the sampling trajectory into state-of-the-art training-free accuracy, with gains largest out of domain and on the harder splits -- exactly where likelihood alone falls short -- at single-digit-percent wall-clock overhead. DEGS narrowly trails an in-house GRPO reference on the math splits GRPO was trained for, yet surpasses it out of domain on GPQA for all three models, without any training, reward model, or labeled data.

</details>


### [256] [Low-Rank Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09694)
*Jonathan Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出低秩注意力残差（LR-AttnRes），通过使用低维键（r << d）进行深度路由，解耦路由与残差表示，在减少计算量的同时提升LLM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Attention Residuals将每个子层输出同时作为全维度的键和值，导致路由与表示耦合，且深度路由分数随隐藏层宽度d缩放，计算效率低。

Method: 提出两种变体：(1) Projected LR-AttnRes：从现有输出投影中学习低秩键（r维），解耦路由与残差内容；(2) Sliced LR-AttnRes：直接取每个值的最后r个维度作为路由键，消除辅助键投影路径，减少FLOPs。

Result: 实验证明深度路由在远低于模型宽度的维度下仍然有效；Projected LR-AttnRes在验证损失上达到最佳；Sliced LR-AttnRes在减少残差侧FLOPs的同时仍能提升性能。

Conclusion: 低秩路由机制有效解耦了路由与表示，降低了计算成本，为LLM中注意力残差设计提供了更高效的新方案。

Abstract: Attention Residuals replace the fixed residual sum with depthwise attention over previous sub-layer outputs in large language models (LLMs), but use each output as both a full-dimensional key and value. This couples routing with representation and makes depth-routing scores scale with the hidden width $d$. We propose Low-Rank Attention Residuals (LR-AttnRes), which keep full-dimensional residual values while using $r$-dimensional keys, with $r \ll d$, for routing. Projected LR-AttnRes emits learned low-rank keys from existing output projections, decoupling routing from residual content and achieving the best validation loss among the variants tested. Sliced LR-AttnRes uses the last $r$ dimensions of each value as the routing key, removing the auxiliary key-projection path and reducing residual-side FLOPs while still improving performance. Comprehensive sweeps show that depthwise routing can be effective with far fewer dimensions than the model width. We release code and models to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [257] [FedCausal-Dyn: A Causal-Dynamic Paradigm for Federated Learning under Dynamic Feature Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09695)
*Kaijie Chen,Alex Johnson,Maria Garcia,Wei Zhang,Daniel Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCausal-Dyn框架，通过因果-动态范式解决联邦学习中动态特征漂移问题，利用因果域特征分离、动态原型聚合和因果特征引导协同正则化，在三个基准数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法假设静态漂移，无法有效应对真实应用中数据分布随客户端和时间动态演化的非平稳环境（如金融科技领域）。

Method: 1) 因果域特征分离：通过专用投影头和对抗训练分离域不变因果特征与域特定虚假变化；2) 动态原型聚合：基于估计可靠性加权聚合局部类原型；3) 因果特征引导协同正则化：统一原型对比对齐与域不变性为一致目标。

Result: 在三个联邦域泛化基准上，FedCausal-Dyn持续达到最佳平均准确率与最稳定结果，消融实验验证了各模块的关键贡献。

Conclusion: FedCausal-Dyn为动态特征漂移下的联邦学习提供了一个鲁棒且有理论原则的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenging problem of dynamic feature drift in federated learning, where data distributions evolve across clients and over time -- a common scenario in real-world applications like financial technology. Existing approaches often assume static drift, limiting their effectiveness in non-stationary environments. To overcome this, we propose \textbf{FedCausal-Dyn}, a novel federated learning framework built on a causal-dynamic paradigm. Its key innovation is \textit{causal-domain feature separation}, which disentangles domain-invariant causal features from spurious, domain-specific variations via specialized projection heads and adversarial training. This enables \textit{reliable and dynamic prototype aggregation}, weighting local class prototypes by estimated reliability before global aggregation. We further introduce \textit{causal-feature guided collaborative regularization}, unifying prototype contrastive alignment and domain invariance into a cohesive objective. Extensive experiments on three federated domain generalization benchmarks demonstrate that FedCausal-Dyn consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, with the highest average accuracy and the most stable results. Ablation studies confirm each component's critical contribution. Our work provides a robust and principled solution for federated learning under dynamic feature drift.

</details>


### [258] [Mitigating Early Training Collapse in CTR Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09696)
*Ergun Biçici,Erkan Çetinyamaç*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度神经网络点击率预测模型在首个训练周期后验证性能骤降，通过控制特征稀疏性（移除高稀疏特征、聚合低频特征值）可稳定训练、改善离线与在线指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点击率预测的深度神经网络在训练第一个epoch后验证性能急剧下降，这种不稳定性限制了有效学习和模型性能，需要分析其原因并找到实用的缓解策略。

Method: 利用大规模工业数据集分析训练不稳定性行为，对比不同缓解策略：降低学习率（效果有限）与控制特征稀疏性（大幅改进）。具体措施包括移除高稀疏特征和聚合低频特征值。

Result: 降低学习率仅有渐进式改进，而控制特征稀疏性可显著稳定训练过程，使模型能够超越单周期训练继续学习，并同时提升离线评估指标与在线系统性能。

Conclusion: 特征稀疏性是导致CTR模型训练初期验证性能下降的关键因素，通过针对性处理特征稀疏问题可有效克服训练不稳定性，实现更优的模型性能。

Abstract: Deep neural models for click-through rate prediction often exhibit a sharp decline in validation performance immediately after the first training epoch despite continued improvement in training loss. This instability restricts effective learning and limits model performance. In this study, we analyze this behavior using large-scale industrial datasets and evaluate practical mitigation strategies. While reducing the learning rate provides only incremental gains, controlling feature sparsity yields substantial improvements. Removing highly sparse features and aggregating infrequent feature values stabilizes training, extends useful learning beyond a single epoch, and improves both offline evaluation metrics and online system performance.

</details>


### [259] [Safe responses matter: Output-aware safety guardrail mitigate over-refusal in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09697)
*Jiayi Li,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出输出感知的安全护栏（OutGuard），通过在多模态大语言模型的隐藏状态空间中预测即将生成的输出是否不安全，仅在模型实际会产生有害响应时进行干预，从而在保持安全性能的同时大幅减少过度拒绝，解决了安全性与实用性之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM的安全机制存在安全性与实用性之间的根本性权衡：微调方法虽安全但损害通用能力，输入侧安全护栏虽轻量但存在严重的过度拒绝（over-refusal）问题，会错误地拦截本可通过模型自身安全机制无害处理或拒绝/建议回答的良性查询。根本原因在于输入感知范式在不考虑模型自身能否生成安全响应的情况下做出安全决策。

Method: 提出输出感知安全护栏（Output-Aware Safety Guardrail），在模型的隐藏状态空间中预测即将生成的输出是否不安全。通过多实例对比学习（multi-instance contrastive learning）在隐藏状态表示上训练轻量级分类器，区分会导致不安全输出的输入与不会导致不安全输出的输入（即使输入本身包含风险元素），仅在模型实际响应有害时进行精确干预。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提出的输出感知安全护栏在安全性能上与现有方法相当，同时大幅减少了过度拒绝，保留了模型的实用性和内置安全能力。

Conclusion: 从输入感知范式转向输出感知范式可以更有效地利用MLLMs自身已有的内在安全机制（将有害输入转化为无害输出），在保证安全性的前提下避免过度拒绝，从而更好地平衡安全与实用性。

Abstract: Existing safety mechanisms for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) face a fundamental trade-off between safety and utility. Model fine-tuning achieves robust safety but compromises general utility. Input-side safety guardrails offer a lightweight alternative, yet they suffer from severe over-refusal, indiscriminately blocking benign queries or those the model could have safely answered through refusal or advisory responses. We identify that the root cause of over-refusal lies in the input-aware paradigm: safety guardrails make safety decisions without considering whether the model itself is capable of generating safe responses. Usually, MLLMs already possess intrinsic safety mechanisms that can transform harmful inputs into harmless outputs, but input-side safety guardrails override this capability, degrading user experience. Motivated by this insight, we propose a paradigm shift toward output-aware safety guardrails. Our method operates within the model's hidden state space to predict whether the forthcoming generation will be unsafe before it is fully produced. By training a lightweight classifier via multi-instance contrastive learning on hidden state representations, our approach distinguishes between inputs that will lead to unsafe outputs and those that will not, even when the inputs themselves contain risky elements. This enables precise intervention only when the model's actual response would be harmful. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our output-aware safety guardrail matches the safety performance of existing methods while drastically reducing over-refusal, preserving the model's utility and built-in safety capabilities. Code is available at: https://github.com/kunzhan/OutGuard

</details>


### [260] [Quantum-Inspired Contextual Learning for Sparse-Ring Fraud Detection in Dynamic Transaction Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09704)
*Behnam Tonekaboni,Hiroshi Yamauchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对动态金融交易图中的欺诈检测问题，提出了一个探索性基准测试和量子启发式建模原型，重点关注跨时间步分布的稀疏环欺诈模式。实验表明，仅使用拓扑摘要无法完成监督环补全任务，而结合身份保留图特征与拓扑摘要的混合表示效果最佳，且上下文机器学习（CML）是处理时间与关系上下文分布证据的有前景模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调型欺诈在单笔交易中不可见，而是表现为跨多个时间周期的关系模式。现有模型难以有效整合时间和图结构两方面的证据来检测这种分布式欺诈模式。

Method: 使用合成交易模拟器生成包含完整稀疏环和破坏环诱饵的数据集；将每日有向交易图聚合成滚动窗口，提取原始图特征、持续同调摘要或混合特征向量；采用门控循环单元（GRU）和量子启发式上下文机器学习（CML）作为序列级分类器进行对比。

Result: 仅拓扑摘要因去除了账户对身份和边方向信息，过于压缩而无法解决监督环补全任务；混合表示（图特征+拓扑摘要）效果最强；CML在处理跨时间和关系上下文的证据分布时表现有潜力。

Conclusion: 拓扑信息最适合作为动态图特征的上下文层使用，CML是处理时间与关系上下文分布证据的欺诈模式的有前景候选模型。该研究为探索性基准，样本量有限，结论需进一步验证。

Abstract: We present an exploratory benchmark and quantum-inspired modeling prototype for fraud screening in dynamic financial transaction graphs.
